\begin{array}[]{rl}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\int_{c}^{x}\frac{1}{\zeta^{2}}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}}-1\right)d\zeta&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\int_{c}^{x}\frac{2}{\zeta^{2}}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}}-1\right)d\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{2}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\int_{\frac{c}{\sqrt{2}}}^{\frac{x}{\sqrt{2}}}\frac{1}{\zeta^{2}}\left(e^{\zeta^{2}}-1\right)d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{x}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}x^{2}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{c}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}c^{2}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{\frac{c}{\sqrt{2}}}^{\frac{x}{\sqrt{2}}}e^{\zeta^{2}}d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{x}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}x^{2}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{c}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}c^{2}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{2}\frac{2}{\sqrt{\pi}}\int_{\frac{c}{\sqrt{2}}}^{\frac{x}{\sqrt{2}}}e^{\zeta^{2}}d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{1}{x}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}x^{2}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{2}{\rm erfi}\left(\frac{x}{\sqrt{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left({\rm erfi}\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{2}{c}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}c^{2}}-1\right)\right)\\
\end{array}

So:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}\left|a^{[{\rm ReLU}]}_{k}\right|x^{k}&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{x^{2p}}{(2p-1)2^{p}p!}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}x\mathop{\sum}\limits_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{x^{2p-1}}{(2p-1)2^{p}p!}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}x\int_{c}^{x}\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial\zeta}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{\zeta^{2p-1}}{(2p-1)2^{p}p!}\right)d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}x\int_{c}^{x}\left(\mathop{\sum}\limits_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{\zeta^{2p-2}}{2^{p}p!}\right)d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2\pi}}x\int_{c}^{x}\left(\mathop{\sum}\limits_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{p!}\left(\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\right)^{p-1}\right)d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}x\int_{c}^{x}\frac{1}{\zeta^{2}}\left(\mathop{\sum}\limits_{p=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{p!}\left(\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}\right)^{p}\right)d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}x\int_{c}^{x}\frac{1}{\zeta^{2}}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{2}}-1\right)d\zeta\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x\left({\rm erfi}\left(\frac{x}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+1-{\rm erfi}\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{2}{c}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}c^{2}}-1\right)\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(1-e^{\frac{1}{2}x^{2}}\right)\\
\end{array}

Select
c
so that the first derivative is
\frac{1}{2}x
:

\begin{array}[]{rl}-{\rm erfi}\left(\frac{c}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\frac{2}{c}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}c^{2}}-1\right)=0\mbox{ if }c=0\end{array}

Hence:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\rm ReLU}]}\left(x\right)&\!\!\!\!\triangleq\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}\left|a^{[{\rm ReLU}]}_{k}\right|\left(1+x\right)^{k}-\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}\left|a^{[{\rm ReLU}]}_{k}\right|\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}\left(1+x\right)\left({\rm erfi}\left(\frac{1+x}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+1\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(1-e^{\frac{1}{2}\left(1+x\right)^{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\left({\rm erfi}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+1\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(1-e^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{2}x\left({\rm erfi}\left(\frac{1+x}{\sqrt{2}}\right)+1\right)+\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\left(e^{\frac{1}{2}}-e^{\frac{1}{2}\left(1+x\right)^{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\left({\rm erfi}\left(\frac{1+x}{\sqrt{2}}\right)-{\rm erfi}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\right)\right)\\
\end{array}

(28)

Figure 3:
The ReLU magnitude activation and associated
functions - ReLU activation
\tau^{[{\rm ReLU}]}
(top left),
magnitude
{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\rm ReLU}]}
(top right), rectified
activation
{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\rm ReLU}]}
for various
\xi,\xi^{\prime}
pairs
(bottom left) and its envelope (bottom right) for
\xi,\xi^{\prime}\in[-2,2]
. Note that
\eta=0.75
for all plots.

Appendix B
Proof of the Global Dual Model

Here we prove the validity of the global dual model presented in the
paper. Recall that the dual model has the form (Theorem
1
,
equation (
10
)):

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\bf f}\left({\bf x};{\Theta}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf g}}\\
\end{array}

(29)

where, as per Figure
2
,

{{\bm{\Psi}}}={{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[D-1]}
,

{{\bm{\phi}}}={{\bm{\phi}}}^{[D-1]}
,

{\bf g}={\bf g}^{[D-1]}
and, given the base case

{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}({{\Theta}})={\bf 1}_{n}
,

{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}({\bf x})={\bf x}
,

{\bf g}^{[-1]}={\bf 1}_{n}
, the recursive definition of the feature
maps and metric is proposed:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\left|a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}\!\left[{\binom{k}{l}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\!\left(\sqrt{\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}}{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]\left<\frac{1}{2}\right>}\!\left[{\binom{k}{l}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\!\left(\sqrt{\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}}{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
{\tilde{\bf g}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\left[{\rm He}_{k-l}{\bf g}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\end{array}

(30)

\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}

(the feature map transforms associated with the edges of the graph) and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}+{{\bm{\upsilon}}}_{\tau}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right){\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\gamma}\\
\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\left[\frac{1}{{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{{\widetilde{{\bm{\Phi}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{\bf g}^{[j]}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}1\\
\left[{\tilde{\bf g}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\end{array}\right]\end{array}

(31)

(the feature map transforms associated with the nodes of the graph) where

{{\bm{\upsilon}}}_{\tau}^{[j]}=\sum_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)}{{\gamma}}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}
. Our approach to demonstrating that this is true is to
prove that, given some input
{\bf x}\in\mathbb{X}
then, for all edges

({\widetilde{\jmath}}\to j)
:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}-{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(0\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\tilde{\bf g}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\\
\end{array}

(32)

and likewise for all nodes
j
:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\bf x}^{[j]}&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf g}^{[j]}}\\
\end{array}

(33)

Base case:
By the definition of the base case, we have:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\bf x}^{[-1]}&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf g}^{[-1]}}={\bf x}\\
\end{array}

Node case:
Assume (
32
) is true. Then, using
(
31
), we have that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf g}^{[j]}}\par&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}{\bf b}^{[j]}+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\left({\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(0\right)+{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}-{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(0\right)\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}{\bf b}^{[j]}+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\bf x}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}

Edge case:
Assume (
33
) is true. Then, using
(
30
), we have that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left<{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\tilde{\bf g}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\left<{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\tilde{\bf g}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)^{\otimes l},{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{{\bf g}^{[j]\otimes l}}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf g}^{[j]}}^{l}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}{x}_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]l}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 0}a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{0\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}{x}_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]l}-\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 0}a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{0\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}0^{l}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({x}_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)-\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(0\right)\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}-{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(0\right)\\
\end{array}

The desired result (
29
) then follows by identifying the
output node
j=D-1
.

B.1
Norm-Bounds for the Global Dual Model

Our proof of the norm-bounds of the global model follows the same model as our
proof of the validity of said model. We want to prove the bounds

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\in\left[{\tilde{\phi}}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right),{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right]\\
\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq{\tilde{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{\psi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\\
\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={\!\begin{array}[]{c}\!\!\!\!\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{{{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\atop{-\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}}}\end{array}\!}\left\{\frac{\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)^{2}}{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)}\right\}\\
\end{array}

(34)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\in\left[{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[j]2}={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{{\tilde{\phi}}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}},{\phi}^{[j]2}={\gamma}^{2}+1\right]\\
\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq{\psi}^{[j]2}=\left({\beta}^{[j]}+\frac{{\mu}^{[j]}\left|\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)\right|}{{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}{\mu}^{[j]2}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[j]2}{\tilde{\psi}}^{[j]2}\\
\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[j]2}=\left({\beta}^{[j]}+\frac{{\mu}^{[j]}\left|\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)\right|}{{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}{\mu}^{[j]2}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[j]2}\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[j]2}\\
\end{array}

(35)

with the base-cases

\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}({\bf x})\|_{2}^{2}\in[{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[-1]2}=0,{\phi}^{[-1]2}=1]
,

\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}({\Theta})\|_{2}^{2}\leq{\psi}^{[-1]2}=1

and

\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}({\Theta})\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}\leq\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[-1]2}=1
.
We proceed as follows:

Base case:
By the definition of the base case, using our assumptions, we
have:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|{\bf x}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq 1={\phi}^{[-1]2}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|{\bf I}_{n}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq 1={\psi}^{[-1]2}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\rm sup}\limits_{{\bf x}\in\mathbb{X}}\left\|\left<\frac{{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)}{\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}},{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right]_{{\bf g}}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathop{\rm sup}\limits_{{\bf x}\in\mathbb{X}}\left\|\frac{{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)}{\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq 1=\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[-1]2}\end{array}

Node case:
Assume (
34
) is true. Then, using our assumptions,
we have that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\gamma}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\end{array}\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\left\|{{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\in\left[{\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{{\tilde{\phi}}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}={\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[j]2},{\gamma}^{2}+1={\phi}^{[j]2}\right]\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}+{{\bm{\upsilon}}}_{\tau}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
\sqrt{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right){\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}\right]\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\left(\left\|{\bf b}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{{\bm{\upsilon}}}_{\tau}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right){\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\left({\beta}^{[j]}+\frac{{\mu}^{[j]}\left|\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)\right|}{{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{\widetilde{{\psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\left({\beta}^{[j]}+\frac{{\mu}^{[j]}\left|\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)\right|}{{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[j]}{\widetilde{{\psi}}}^{[j]2}{\mu}^{[j]2}={\psi}^{[j]2}\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}+{{\bm{\upsilon}}}_{\tau}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
\sqrt{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right){\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}\right]\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\left(\left\|{\bf b}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{{\bm{\upsilon}}}_{\tau}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right){\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\left({\beta}^{[j]}+\frac{{\mu}^{[j]}\left|\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)\right|}{{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\undertilde{{\widetilde{{\psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\left({\beta}^{[j]}+\frac{{\mu}^{[j]}\left|\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(0)\right|}{{\gamma}}\right)^{2}+{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[j]}\undertilde{{\widetilde{{\psi}}}}^{[j]2}{\mu}^{[j]2}=\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[j]2}\\
\end{array}

Edge case:
Assume (
35
) is true. Then, using
our assumptions and the definition (
6
), and using that the
magnitude function is increasing on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
, we have that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|\left[a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]\left<\frac{1}{2}\right>}\!\left[{\binom{k}{l}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\!\left(\sqrt{\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}}{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\left|a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right|\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}\left(\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{l}\\
&\!\!\!\!={{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\in\left[{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right),{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\in\left[{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)={\tilde{\phi}}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2},{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right]\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\max}\limits_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\left\|\left[\left|a_{(0)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right|^{\frac{1}{2}}\!\left[{\binom{k}{l}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\!\left(\sqrt{\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}}{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\max}\limits_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!={{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{\overline{s}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={\!\begin{array}[]{c}\!\!\!\!\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{{{\phi}_{{{\downarrow}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\atop{-\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\leq\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}}}\end{array}\!}\left\{\frac{\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)^{2}}{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)}\right\}\\
\end{array}

where the final bound in this sequence is simply the definition of the norm in
question with the range of the supremum expanded to the known bound on this
range.

The desired result follows by identifying the output node
j=D-1
. We note
that the bounds
{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
may be chosen
arbitrarily here.

Appendix C
Proof of the Local Dual Model

We now repeat the proof from the previous section
B
for
the local model. Recall that the dual model has the form (Theorem

1
, equation (
10
)):

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf f}\left({\bf x};{\Delta}{\Theta}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf G}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{D-1}}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{D-1}}\left({\bf x}\right)}\right]_{i_{D-1}}\\
\end{array}

(36)

where, as per Figure
2
,

{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}={{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}
,

{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}={{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}
,

{\bf G}_{\Delta}={\bf G}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}
and,
given the base case

{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}({\Delta}{{\Theta}})={\bf 0}_{0\times n}
,

{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}({\bf x})={\bf 0}_{0}

{\bf G}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}({\bf x})={\bf 1}_{0\times n}

the recursive definition of the feature maps is proposed as:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\frac{1}{\eta^{k}}\left[\left(\sqrt{\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[{\eta^{k}}\left[\left(\sqrt{\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}}{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left[\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
\end{array}

(37)

\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}

(the feature map transforms associated with the edges of the graph) and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\gamma}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
\sqrt{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\bf I}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\right){\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\sqrt{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right){\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\sqrt{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right){\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{\bf G}^{[j]}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}^{\rm T}\\
\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\bf I}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\right){\bf 1}_{{\tilde{H}}^{[j]}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[j]}}^{\rm T}\\
\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\widetilde{\bf G}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)\right){\bf 1}_{{\tilde{H}}^{[j]}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[j]}}^{\rm T}\\
\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\widetilde{\bf G}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)\right){\bf 1}_{{\tilde{H}}^{[j]}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[j]}}^{\rm T}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
\end{array}

(38)

(the feature map transforms associated with the nodes of the graph). Our
approach to demonstrating that this is true is to prove that, given some input

{\bf x}\in\mathbb{X}
then, for all edges
({\widetilde{\jmath}}\to j)
:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)}\\
\end{array}

(39)

and likewise for all nodes
j
:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf x}^{[j]}&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf G}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)}\\
\end{array}

(40)

Base case:
By the definition of the base case, we have:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf x}^{[-1]}&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf G}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)}={\bf 0}_{n}\\
\end{array}

Node case:
Assume (
39
) is true. Then, using
(
38
), we have that:

\begin{array}[]{l}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\bf G}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)}\par={\gamma}{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]}+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\left({\Delta}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}+{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}+{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right)\\
={\gamma}\left({\bf b}^{[j]}+{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]}\right)+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\left({\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}+{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right)^{\rm T}\left({\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}+{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right)-{\gamma}{\bf b}^{[j]}-\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\\
=\left({\bf x}^{[j]}+{\Delta}{\bf x}^{[j]}\right)-{\bf x}^{[j]}={\Delta}{\bf x}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}

Edge case:
Assume (
40
) is true. Then, using
(
37
) and (
27
), we have that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left<{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)}^{l}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}{\Delta}{x}_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[j]l}\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[{\overline{\tau}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Delta}{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[j]};{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}+{\Delta}{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[j]}\right)-\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\right]_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\\
\end{array}

The desired result (
36
) then follows by identifying the
output node
j=D-1
.

C.1
Norm-Bounds for the Local Dual Model

Our proof of the norm-bounds of the local model follows the same model as our
proof of the validity of said model. We want to prove the bounds

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq{\tilde{\phi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={{{T}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\forall i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}\\
\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{\psi}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\\
\end{array}

(41)

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{j}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq{\phi}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}={\gamma}^{2}+1\forall i_{j}\\
\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!\leq{\psi}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}={\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\left({\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j]2}+2{\mu}^{[j]2}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}\right)\\
\end{array}

(42)

with the base-cases

\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}({\bf x})\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{-1}}^{[-1]}({\bf x})\|_{2}^{2}\leq{\phi}_{\Delta}^{[-1]2}=0

and

\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}({\Delta}{\Theta})\|_{2}^{2}\leq{\psi}_{\Delta}^{[-1]2}=0
.
We proceed as follows:

Base case:
By the definition of the base case, using our assumptions, we
have:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{-1}}^{[-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=0={\phi}^{[-1]2}\forall i_{-1}\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[-1]}\left({\Delta}{\Theta}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=0={\psi}^{[-1]2}\end{array}

Node case:
Assume (
41
) is true. Then, using our assumptions,
we have that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{j}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\left\|{\bf x}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\frac{2}{3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{\gamma}^{2}+1={\phi}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\Delta}{\Theta}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}\right\|_{2}^{2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\left\|{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}^{2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\left\|{\bf W}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}^{2}+\ldots\\
&\hfill\ldots 3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\left\|{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j]}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}{\mu}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\mathop{\max}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j]2}{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+6{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}{\mu}^{[j]2}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\left({\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j]2}+2{\mu}^{[j]2}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}\right)={\psi}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]2}\\
\end{array}

Edge case:
Assume (
42
) is true. Then, using
our assumptions and the definition (
7
) of the rectified
activation function and it’s increasing (on
\mathbb{R}_{+}
) envelope, we have
that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\left\|\left[\frac{a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{\eta^{k}}\left[\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\right)^{\frac{l}{2}}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\otimes l}\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left\|\left[\frac{a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k}}\left[\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}\left(\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\right)^{\frac{l}{2}}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{\eta^{2k}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}^{2}{\rm He}_{k-l}^{2}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)^{l}\\
&\!\!\!\!={{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2};{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]},{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2};\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2};\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{{{T}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}={\tilde{\phi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\\
\end{array}

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Delta}{\Theta}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\max}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}\left\|\left[\eta^{k}\left[\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\right)^{\frac{l}{2}}\left({{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\right\|_{2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\max}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}{{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!={{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq{{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)={\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\\
\end{array}

(43)

The desired result follows by identifying the output node
j=D-1
.

C.2
Convergence Region of Local Dual Model

Here we prove the bounds on the change in weights and biases for which the
local dual model holds as state in theorem
5
. The
goal is to place bounds on
{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]},{\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]}

to ensure that:

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\leq{{{{R}}}}_{\eta}^{2}\end{array}

\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}
,
\forall j
in
(
43
). This result suffices to ensure that

\|{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}({\Delta}{\Theta})\|_{{\tilde{\bf h}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]*}}^{2}\leq{{{{S}}}}_{\eta}^{2}
, and subsequently
that both feature maps are convergent, allowing us to conclude that the
model is well-defined (convergent) and enabling e.g. our bound on Rademacher
complexity to be derived.

We aim to find the largest possible
{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]},{\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]}

such that:

\begin{array}[]{l}\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\leq{{{{R}}}}_{\eta}^{2}\end{array}

\forall j:{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}
. So we require that:

\begin{array}[]{l}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\leq\mathop{\min}\limits_{j:{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{R}}}}_{\eta}^{2}\end{array}

(44)

Recall that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\psi}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}={\beta}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\mu}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}+2{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\end{array}

Thus our condition becomes:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left({\beta}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)+6{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\leq{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\end{array}

Selecting
d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\in(0,1)

\forall j
we can simplify the requirement
to:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left({\beta}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\leq d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\end{array}

and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}6{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\leq\left(1-d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\mathop{\min}\limits_{j:{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{R}}}}_{\eta}^{2}\end{array}

Re-ordering, we find:

\begin{array}[]{rl}6{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}{{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}\left(\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}^{\prime},{\widetilde{\jmath}}]})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}^{\prime},{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}^{\prime}]2}\right)\leq\left(1-d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right){{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\;\;\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}^{\prime}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\end{array}

and after cleaning up and re-indexing:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}\leq\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]})\eta}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{6{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}\left(1-d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right){{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\;\;\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}:j\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\end{array}

so, overall:

\begin{array}[]{l}{{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}\leq\mathop{\min}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}:j\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]})\eta}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\{{{{{R}}}}_{\eta}^{2},{{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{6{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}\left(1-d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right){{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\right\}\end{array}

and so sufficient conditions are:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left({\beta}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}+3{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j]2}{\mu}_{\Delta}^{[j]2}\right)\leq d^{[j]}u^{[j]2}\end{array}

where:

\begin{array}[]{l}u^{[j]2}=\mathop{\min}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}:j\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]})\eta}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left\{{{{{R}}}}_{\eta}^{2},{{{\hat{s}}}}_{\eta}^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{6{\tilde{p}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}\left(1-d^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right){{\psi}}_{\Delta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)\right\}\end{array}

and the final result follows by setting
d^{[j]}=\frac{1}{2}

\forall j
.

C.3
RKHS Form of Local Model

Our goal in this section is to derive the LiNK kernel from the Local dual
model. For reason that will become apparent we find it convenient to work
with a slight variant of the local dual feature map with minor re-scaling,
namely:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\left[\left(\sqrt{\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\left[\left(\sqrt{\frac{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}}{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left[\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\otimes l}\right]_{1\leq l\leq k}\right]_{k\geq 1}\\
\end{array}

(45)

\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}
and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\gamma}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\frac{1}{\sqrt{2{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[j]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
\sqrt{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\bf I}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\right){\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\sqrt{2{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\sqrt{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right){\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\sqrt{2{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\sqrt{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\widetilde{{\bm{\Psi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\Theta}\right)\right){\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{\bf G}^{[j]}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf 1}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}^{\rm T}\\
\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\bf I}_{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\right){\bf 1}_{{\tilde{H}}^{[j]}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[j]}}^{\rm T}\\
\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\widetilde{\bf G}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)\right){\bf 1}_{{\tilde{H}}^{[j]}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[j]}}^{\rm T}\\
\mathop{\rm diag}\limits_{{{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}}}\left({\widetilde{\bf G}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}\right)\right){\bf 1}_{{\tilde{H}}^{[j]}}{\bf 1}_{H^{[j]}}^{\rm T}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
\end{array}

(46)

We have already demonstrated that the necessary conditions for functions in
our space to like in an RKHS, so it remains derive the kernel representing
this space. To this end we recall that Reisz representor theory implies that

\forall{\bf x}\in\mathbb{X}

\exists
unique
{\bf K}_{\bf x}\in\mathcal{F}\times\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that
\left<{\bf f}({\bf x}),{\bf v}\right>=\left<{\bf f},{\bf K}_{\bf x}{\bf v}\right>_{\mathcal{H}}

\forall{\bf v}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
; from which the kernel may be obtained
using:

\begin{array}[]{l}{\bf K}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)=\Big[\left<{\bf K}_{\bf x}{{\bm{\delta}}}{}_{(i_{D-1})}^{[D-1]},{\bf K}_{{\bf x}^{\prime}}{{\bm{\delta}}}{}_{(i^{\prime}_{D-1})}^{[D-1]}\right>_{\mathcal{H}}\Big]{}_{{{}\atop{\!i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}}}\end{array}

where
{{\bm{\delta}}}_{(k)}^{[j]}=[\delta_{k,i_{j}}]_{i_{j}}
. Thus our first
task is to find such a
{\bf K}_{\bf x}
. Recall:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf f}\left({\bf x}\right)=\left({{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)^{\rm T}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\end{array}

and so:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left<{\Delta}{\bf f}\left({\bf x}\right),{\bf v}\right>&\!\!\!\!={\Delta}{\bf f}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\rm T}{\bf v}\\
&\!\!\!\!={{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)^{\rm T}\left({{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}^{[D-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right){\bf v}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\left({{\Psi}}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right)v_{i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{{\Psi}}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\left({{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)v_{i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{{\Psi}}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\left(\sum_{i^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\delta_{i^{\prime}_{D-1},i^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)v_{i^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\bf f},{\bf K}_{\bf x}{\bf v}\right>\end{array}

where we have denoted:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\bf f}&\!\!\!\!=\left[{{\Psi}}_{{\Delta}k,i_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right]_{(k,i_{D-1})}\\
{\bf K}_{\bf x}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\delta_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{(k,i_{D-1}),i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\\
\end{array}

We may therefore proceed to derive the kernel as follows, letting

\left<\cdot,\cdot\right>_{\mathcal{H}}
be the standard inner product:

\begin{array}[]{rl}K_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\Bigg[\Bigg<\left[\delta_{\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1},\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{(k,\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1}),\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\left[\delta_{i_{D-1},\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\right]_{\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}},\\
&\left[\delta_{\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1},\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right]_{(k,\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1}),\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\left[\delta_{i^{\prime}_{D-1},\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\right]_{\tilde{i}^{\prime\prime}_{D-1}}\Bigg>_{\mathcal{H}}\Bigg]_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\Bigg[\Bigg<\left[\delta_{\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1},i_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\right]_{(k,\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1})},\\
&\left[\delta_{\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right]_{(k,\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1})}\Bigg>_{\mathcal{H}}\Bigg]_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k,\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1}}\delta_{\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1},i_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right)\delta_{\tilde{i}^{\prime}_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right]_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left[\delta_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}\right){{\phi}}_{{\Delta}k}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right){G}_{{\Delta}k,i^{\prime}_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right]_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\delta_{i_{D-1},i^{\prime}_{D-1}}{K}_{i_{D-1}}^{[D-1]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\\
\end{array}

where:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{K}_{i_{j}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left<{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{j}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{j}}^{[j]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right>\\
&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left<{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]},{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right>+\ldots\\
&\;\;\;\;\ldots+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}\left<{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}\right),{\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\odot{\widetilde{\bf G}}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right>\\
&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left<{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]},{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right>+\ldots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\ldots+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{2k}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}^{2}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}\left<{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\odot{\bf G}_{{\Delta}:i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right>\right)^{l}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left<{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]},{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right>+\ldots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\ldots+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\frac{1}{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}a_{\big({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\big)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{2k}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{1\leq l\leq k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}^{2}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{K}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{l}\\
&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\left<{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]},{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\right>+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\frac{1}{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{K}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right);{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]},{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\\
\end{array}

So, noting that this is independent of
i_{j}
, we find that
{\bf f}\in\mathcal{H}_{\bf K}
, where
{\bf K}={\bf I}K_{\rm LiNK}
, where

K_{\rm LiNK}=K_{\rm LiNK}^{[D-1]}
is defined recursively:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\Sigma^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\frac{1}{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right);{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]},{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\\
\end{array}

with
{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[-1]}({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime})=0
.

Recall that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{a_{(\xi)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}a_{(\xi^{\prime})k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{2k}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l=1}^{k}{\binom{k}{l}}^{2}{\rm He}_{k-l}^{2}\zeta^{l}\\
\end{array}

We aim to express:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{q}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta,q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)\end{array}

where
{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta,q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime})=b(\xi,\xi^{\prime})\zeta^{q}
. As
noted previously, if:

\begin{array}[]{rll}f\left(x\right)&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}a_{k}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l=0}^{k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}x^{l}\\
\end{array}

then:

\begin{array}[]{rll}f^{(q)}\left(x\right)&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{(k+q)!}{k!}a_{k+q}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l=0}^{k-q}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}x^{l}\\
\end{array}

Working toward the general case:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta,1}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\zeta\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{a_{(\xi)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}a_{(\xi^{\prime})k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{2k}}{\binom{k}{1}}^{2}{\rm He}_{k-1}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\zeta\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{a_{(\xi)k+1}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k+1}}{\binom{k+1}{1}}{\rm He}_{k}\frac{a_{(\xi^{\prime})k^{\prime}+1}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k^{\prime}+1}}{\binom{k^{\prime}+1}{1}}{\rm He}_{k^{\prime}}\\
\end{array}

and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta,2}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\zeta^{2}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{a_{(\xi)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}a_{(\xi^{\prime})k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{2k}}{\binom{k}{2}}^{2}{\rm He}_{k-2}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\zeta^{2}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{a_{(\xi)k+2}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k+2}}{\binom{k+2}{2}}{\rm He}_{k}\frac{a_{(\xi^{\prime})k^{\prime}+2}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k^{\prime}+2}}{\binom{k^{\prime}+2}{2}}{\rm He}_{k^{\prime}}\\
\end{array}

and so on, so:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta,q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\zeta^{q}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=q}^{\infty}\frac{a_{(\xi)k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}a_{(\xi^{\prime})k}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{qk}}{\binom{k}{q}}^{2}{\rm He}_{k-q}^{2}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\zeta^{q}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{a_{(\xi)k+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k+q}}{\binom{k+q}{q}}{\rm He}_{k}\frac{a_{(\xi^{\prime})k^{\prime}+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k^{\prime}+q}}{\binom{k^{\prime}+q}{q}}{\rm He}_{k^{\prime}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{q!^{2}}\zeta^{q}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k,k^{\prime}}\frac{a_{(\xi)k+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k+q}}\frac{(k+q)!}{k!}{\rm He}_{k}\frac{a_{(\xi^{\prime})k^{\prime}+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k^{\prime}+q}}\frac{(k^{\prime}+q)!}{k^{\prime}!}{\rm He}_{k^{\prime}}\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{q!^{2}}\zeta^{q}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k,k^{\prime}=0}^{\infty}\delta_{k,k^{\prime}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l=0}^{k-q}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l^{\prime}=0}^{k^{\prime}-q}\delta_{l,l^{\prime}}\left(\frac{a_{(\xi)k+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k+q}}\frac{(k+q)!}{k!}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}0^{l}\right)\left(\frac{a_{(\xi^{\prime})k^{\prime}+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k^{\prime}+q}}\frac{(k^{\prime}+q)!}{k^{\prime}!}\binom{k^{\prime}}{l^{\prime}}{\rm He}_{k^{\prime}-l^{\prime}}0^{l^{\prime}}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\zeta^{q}\left(\frac{1}{q!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{a_{(\xi)k+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k+q}}\frac{(k+q)!}{k!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l=0}^{k-q}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}0^{l}\right)\cdot\left(\frac{1}{q!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k^{\prime}=0}^{\infty}\frac{a_{(\xi^{\prime})k^{\prime}+q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}{\eta^{k^{\prime}+q}}\frac{(k^{\prime}+q)!}{k^{\prime}!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l^{\prime}=0}^{k^{\prime}-q}\binom{k^{\prime}}{l^{\prime}}{\rm He}_{k^{\prime}-l^{\prime}}0^{l^{\prime}}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{q!}{\overline{\tau}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left(0;\xi\right)\frac{1}{q!}{\overline{\tau}}_{\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left(0;\xi^{\prime}\right)\zeta^{q}\\
\end{array}

where
\cdot
is the Cauchy product and in the final step we have used
Mertens’ theorem for Cauchy products, and:

\begin{array}[]{rll}{\overline{\tau}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}_{\eta}\left(\zeta;\xi\right)&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k=1}^{\infty}\frac{a^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}_{(\xi)k}}{\eta^{k}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l=1}^{k}\binom{k}{l}{\rm He}_{k-l}\zeta^{l}\\
\end{array}

Finally, we see that, in the limit
\eta\to 1
:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{{\hat{\tau}}}}_{\eta,q}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left(\zeta;\xi,\xi^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{q!}{\overline{\tau}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left(0;\xi\right)\frac{1}{q!}{\overline{\tau}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left(0;\xi^{\prime}\right)\zeta^{q}\\
\end{array}

which leads to the simplified form of the LiNK:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\Sigma^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)+\ldots\\
&\ldots+\frac{1}{{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\frac{1}{H^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{q=1}^{\infty}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}\frac{1}{q!}{\overline{\tau}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left(0;{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\frac{1}{q!}{\overline{\tau}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left(0;{x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\left(\frac{{{{{\rho}}}}_{(\tilde{{\omega}})\eta}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}{{\gamma}^{2}+1}{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right)^{q}\\
\end{array}

with
{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[-1]}({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime})=0
. With some cleanup:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+\mathbb{E}_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\left[\frac{1}{\tilde{{\omega}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}}\Sigma^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\right]+\ldots\\
&\ldots+\mathbb{E}_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}\left[\frac{1}{{\tilde{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{q=1}^{\infty}\mathbb{E}_{i_{\widetilde{\jmath}}}\left[\frac{1}{q!}\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\frac{1}{q!}\tau^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j](q)}\left({x}_{i_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}^{\prime[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\right)\right]{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime}\right)^{q}\right]\\
\end{array}

with
{K}_{\rm LiNK}^{[-1]}({\bf x},{\bf x}^{\prime})=0
.

Appendix D
Rademacher Complexity Bounds - Proof of Theorems
3
,
4
and
7

In this supplementary we prove theorems relating to the Rademacher complexity
of neural networks for the global and local models.

Theorem
3

The set
\mathcal{F}=\{f(\cdot;{\Theta}):\mathbb{R}^{n}\to\mathbb{R}|{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}\}
of networks (
1
)
satisfying our assumptions has Rademacher complexity bounded by

\mathcal{R}_{N}(\mathcal{F})\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}{{\phi}}\undertilde{{\psi}}

(definitions as per Figure
2
).

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{F}
be the set of attainable neural networks (scalar output) and

\epsilon
a Rademacher random variable. Let
{\bf x}\sim\nu
. Then the
Rademacher complexity is bounded as:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathcal{R}_{N}\left(\mathcal{F}\right)&\!\!\!\!\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}f\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=^{\rm{Global\;Dual}}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}}\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}\left({{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right]_{\bf g}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}\left({{\Theta}}\right),\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right]_{\bf g}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Operator\;norm}}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}\left({{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{{\rm He}[\tau]}\sqrt{\left\|\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Norm\;Bound}}\frac{\undertilde{{\psi}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\left\|\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Jensen}}\frac{\undertilde{{\psi}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left\|\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=^{\{\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\epsilon_{k}\epsilon_{l}=\delta_{k,l}\}}\frac{\undertilde{{\psi}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\sqrt{\sum_{k}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Norm\;Bound}}\frac{\undertilde{{\psi}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\sqrt{N{{\phi}}^{2}}\right]=\frac{{{\phi}}\undertilde{{\psi}}}{\sqrt{N}}\end{array}

∎

Theorem
4

Let
\mathcal{F}=\{f(\cdot;{\Theta}):\mathbb{R}^{n}\to\mathbb{R}|{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}\}
be the set of unbiased networks
(
1
) with
L
-Lipschitz activations satisfying our
assumptions. Then

\mathcal{R}_{N}(\mathcal{F})\leq\max_{\mathcal{S}\in\mathbb{S}}\prod_{j\in\mathcal{S}}L^{2}{\tilde{p}}^{[j]}{\mu}^{[j]}
, where
\mathbb{S}

is the set of all input-output paths in the network graph.

Proof.

Observe from Figure
2
that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}&\!\!\!\!\leq\frac{{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right){\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)}L^{2}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\\
\end{array}

Note that the numerator of the factional part is linearly increasing while the
denominator is superlinearly increasing, so we may pessimise this bound as:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}&\!\!\!\!\leq\frac{{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right){\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)}L^{2}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq\mathop{\lim}\limits_{{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\to 0}\frac{{\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}}{{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\left({{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right){\phi}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\right)}L^{2}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}=\frac{1}{a_{0}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{{{{\overline{s}}}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]-1}\left({\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\right)}L^{2}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\\
\end{array}

As
{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}
is a free parameter here we can let

{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\to 0
, in which limit

{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]2}\leq L^{2}\undertilde{{\psi}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\leq L^{2}p^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}{\mu}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\sum_{{\tilde{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}{\tilde{\phi}}^{[{\tilde{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}},{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}\undertilde{{\tilde{\psi}}}^{[{\tilde{{\widetilde{\jmath}}}},{\widetilde{\jmath}}]2}
.
The result follows recursively, then upper bounding with the pathwise product.
∎

Theorem
7

The set
\mathcal{F}_{\Delta}=\{{\Delta}f(\cdot;{\Delta}{\Theta}):\mathbb{R}^{n}\to\mathbb{R}|{\Delta}{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}_{\Delta}\}
of change in neural-network operation satisfying
(
15
) has Rademacher complexity
\mathcal{R}_{N}(\mathcal{F})\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}}{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}}{{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}
(defined in Figure
2
).

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{F}_{\Delta}
be the set of attainable changes in neural networks
(scalar output) and
\epsilon
a Rademacher random variable. Let
{\bf x}\sim\nu
. Then the Rademacher complexity is bounded as:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\mathcal{R}_{N}\left(\mathcal{F}_{\Delta}\right)&\!\!\!\!\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Delta}f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{\Delta}f\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=^{\rm{Local\;Dual}}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Delta}{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}_{\Delta}}\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right]_{\bf g}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Delta}{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}_{\Delta}}\left<{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right),\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right]_{\bf g}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Cauchy\;Schwarz}}\frac{1}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left[\mathop{\sup}\limits_{{\Delta}{\Theta}\in\mathbb{W}_{\Delta}}\left\|{{\bm{\Psi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\Delta}{{\Theta}}\right)\right\|_{2}\sqrt{\left\|\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Norm\;Bound}}\frac{{{\psi}_{\Delta}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\sqrt{\left\|\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Jensen}}\frac{{{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\left\|\sum_{k}\epsilon_{k}{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=^{\{\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}\epsilon_{k}\epsilon_{l}=\delta_{k,l}\}}\frac{{{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\sqrt{\sum_{k}\left\|{{\bm{\phi}}}_{\Delta}\left({\bf x}_{k}\right)\right\|_{2}^{2}}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!\leq^{\rm{Norm\;Bound}}\frac{{{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}}{N}\mathbb{E}_{\nu}\left[\sqrt{N{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}}^{2}}\right]=\frac{{{\phi}}_{{\Delta}}{{\psi}}_{{\Delta}}}{\sqrt{N}}\end{array}

∎

Appendix E
Derivation of the Exact (LeNK) Representor Theory

In this section we present our derivation of the LeNK-based representor
theory. Our approach to this proof is to start from the first layer (recall
