that we are only concerned with layer-wise networks) and then systematically
feed-forward through the network to the output, at every stage deriving a
representor theory for that layer in terms of an intermediate kernel that
builds off the construction for the previous layer. Then the final
representor theory is simply the intermediate representor theory for the
output of layer
D-1
. To demonstrate the “kernel-ness” at each layer
we construct an explicit feature map from which the kernel (for that layer)
derives using the kernel trick. Before proceeding we note the following:

1.

The feature map necessarily includes the weights and biases, leading
to
kernel warping
as discussed in the body of the paper.

2.

The utility of the feature maps is that the demonstrated (and allow
us to derive) the LeNK. Beyond that their complexity is not a concern.

3.

For purely practical reasons we have chosen to use the indexed tensor
style notation here. This leads to significant notational clutter, but
remove potential ambiguities (at least for the authors) that we found
resulted from premature “cleaning up” of the expressions involved.
Note that we have cleaned up the final formulae in the body of the
paper (post-derivation) to aid readability and interperability.

4.

The layerwise assumption appears to be necessary for kernelisation.
Roughly speaking, in more general case the feedback phase “picks up”
terms from all paths from output node to input node, which subsequently
get fed-forward through all paths, resulting in terms on the
feed-forward process not “pairing up” appropriately with elements
in the nodes (ie. the weights on path
A
influence
{{\bm{\alpha}}}^{[0]}
,
and this effects the change in weights on path
B
, where there are no
corresponding weights with which to connect them through the kernel
trick).

5.

The non-regularized assumption is required to avoid untenable levels of
complexity. We note that it is not difficult to include the effects of
regularisation for a single layer network, but for deeper networks the
number of terms involved grows with each layer and quickly becomes
unmanageable.

To begin, note that by the definition of back-propagation (gradient descent)
in the un-regularized case:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[D-1]}&\!\!\!\!=-\eta\frac{\partial}{\partial{\bf x}_{\{l\}}^{[D-1]}}R\left({\bf x}_{\{l\}}^{[D-1]}-{\bf y}_{\{l\}}\right)\\
{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[j]}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}:j\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}}\tau^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}](1)}\left({\bf x}_{\{l\}}^{[j]}\right)\odot{\tilde{{\bm{\alpha}}}}_{\{l\}}^{[j,{\widetilde{\jmath}}]}\;\;\;\;\forall j\neq D-1\\
{\tilde{{\bm{\alpha}}}}_{\{l\}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}&\!\!\!\!={\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[j]}\;\;\;\;\forall{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}

and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{l}{\tilde{\bf x}}_{\{l\}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]{\rm T}}&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{l}{\gamma}{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[j]{\rm T}}\\
\end{array}

Hence:

{{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf x}^{[j]}&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]}+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}+\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\widetilde{\jmath}}\in{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}}{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]{\rm T}}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[{\widetilde{\jmath}},j]}\\
\end{array}}}

and so, recalling that we are assuming a layer-wise network (ie.

{\widetilde{\mathbb{P}}}^{[j]}=\{j-1\}

\forall j>0
) with

\tau^{[-1,0]}(\zeta)=\zeta
, we have:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf x}^{[j]}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j-1,j]}\\
{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]^{\rm T}}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[j-1,j]}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]+\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf W}^{[j-1,j]}\\
{\bf b}^{[j]^{\rm T}}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[j-1,j]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]+\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[j-1,j]}\\
{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[j]^{\rm T}}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[j-1,j]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]\;\;\forall j>0\end{array}

with the recursive base-case:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\bf x}^{[0]}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}^{[-1,0]}\\
{\Delta}{\bf b}^{[0]^{\rm T}}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[-1,0]}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
\end{array}

To avoid even more notational clutter than that which unavoidably follows, we
define
{\Theta}_{+}={\Theta}+{\Delta}{\Theta}
. Using Einstein the
summation convention:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{x}_{i_{0}}^{[0]}&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{l}{K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l\}i_{0}^{1}}^{[j]}\\
\end{array}

where:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\Sigma^{[0]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime},{\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[-1,0]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\backprime[-1,0]}={\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[0]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)^{\rm T}{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[0]}\left({\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)\\
{K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\prime},{\bf x}^{\backprime};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\delta_{i_{0}}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}\Sigma^{[0]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime},{\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)=\left<{\bf F}^{[0]}{}_{:i_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\bf F}^{[0]}{}^{:i^{\prime}_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\backprime};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\\
\end{array}

and:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[0]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\gamma}\\
{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[-1,0]}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
{\bf F}^{[0]}_{:i_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\delta_{i_{0},i^{\prime}_{0}}\right]_{i^{\prime}_{0}}\otimes{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[0]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\\
\end{array}

Moving forward:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\tilde{x}}^{[0,1]}_{i_{0}}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1}}{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{i_{0}}^{[0]}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{0}^{1}}^{[0]}\par+\ldots\\
\ldots&\!\!\!\!+\frac{1}{2!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1},l_{2}}{{\tau}}^{[0,1](2)}\left({x}_{i_{0}}^{[0]}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{2}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{0}^{1}}^{[0]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2}\}i_{0}^{2}}^{[0]}\par+\ldots\\
\ldots&\!\!\!\!+\frac{1}{3!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1},l_{2},l_{3}}{{\tau}}^{[0,1](3)}\left({x}_{i_{0}}^{[0]}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{2}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{3}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{3}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\ldots\\
&\ldots\;\;\;\;{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{0}^{1}}^{[0]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2}\}i_{0}^{2}}^{[0]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{3}\}i_{0}^{3}}^{[0]}\par+\ldots\\
\ldots&\!\!\!\!+\ldots\end{array}}}

we recall:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[0]}&\!\!\!\!={{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({\bf x}_{\{l\}}^{[0]}\right)\odot{\tilde{{\bm{\alpha}}}}_{\{l\}}^{[0,1]}\\
\end{array}

and so:

{{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\tilde{x}}_{i_{0}}^{[0,1]}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}}\frac{1}{k!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1},l_{2},\ldots,l_{k}}{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1},\ldots,i_{0}^{k}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\bf x}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{0}^{1}}^{[0,1]}{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{2}\}i_{0}^{2}}^{[0,1]}\ldots{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{k}\}i_{0}^{k}}^{[0,1]}\end{array}}}

where:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime},i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;=\left<{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{:i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}^{:i_{0}^{\backprime},i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\end{array}}}

(we require that
k^{\prime}+k^{\prime\prime}+\ldots+k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}=k^{\backprime}+k^{\backprime\backprime}+\ldots+k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}
) and:

{{\begin{array}[]{r}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{:i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)={{\tau}}^{[0,1](k^{\prime})}\left({x}^{\prime[0]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0}}\right){\bf F}^{[0]}{}_{:i^{\prime}_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)^{\otimes k^{\prime}}\otimes\ldots\\
\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](k^{\prime\prime})}\left({x}^{\prime\prime[0]}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}\right){\bf F}^{[0]}{}_{:i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\prime\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)^{\otimes k^{\prime\prime}}\otimes\ldots\otimes{{\tau}}^{[0,1](k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime})}\left({x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime[0]}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\right){\bf F}^{[0]}{}_{:i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left({\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)^{\otimes k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\end{array}}}

from which we observe that:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1},\ldots,i_{0}^{k}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\bf x}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots={{\tau}}^{[0,1](k)}\left({x}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{0}}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{1}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{2}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\ldots{K}^{[0]}{}_{i_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{k}}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l_{k}\}};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{1}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{1}}\right){{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{2}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{2}}\right)\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{k}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{k}}\right)\end{array}}}

as required (the general case can be similarly expanded but the form is
non-trivial as it the kernel splitting depends on the indices
k^{\prime},k^{\prime\prime},\ldots,k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}
and
k^{\backprime},k^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}
).

Recall that:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\widetilde{{\bm{\alpha}}}}}_{\{l\}}^{[0,1]}&\!\!\!\!={\bf W}^{[0,1]}{{{\bm{\alpha}}}}_{\{l\}}^{[1]}\end{array}

It follows that:

{{\begin{array}[]{rl}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}_{:i_{1}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\Delta}b_{i_{1}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{l}{{\alpha}}_{\{l\}i_{1}}^{[1]}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}_{\{l\}}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=\sum_{l}\Sigma^{[1]}\left({\bf x},{\bf x}_{\{l\}}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l\}i_{1}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}}}

and:

{{\begin{array}[]{rl}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf W}_{:i_{1}}^{[0,1]}\\
b_{i_{1}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]&\!\!\!\!={\bf W}^{[1]{\rm T}}_{:i_{1}}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{1}{k!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1},\ldots,l_{k}}R{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{1}^{1}}^{[1]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2}\}i_{1}^{2}}^{[1]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k}\}i_{1}^{k}}^{[1]}\end{array}}}

where:

{{\begin{array}[]{r}R{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{\backprime},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\right)\ldots\\
\begin{array}[]{rl}&\!\!\!\!={W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\ldots\\
&\ldots{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime},i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\bf G}_{K}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right),{\bf G}_{R}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right)\right>\end{array}\\
\end{array}}}

is a tensor-valued kernel, for which:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\bf G}_{K}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\ldots={W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+}\right)\\
{\bf G}_{R}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\ldots={W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}\right)\\
\end{array}}}

Furthermore:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}_{:i_{1}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\Delta}b_{i_{1}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]=\sum_{l}\alpha_{\{l\}i_{1}}^{[1]}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}_{{\mathcal{O}}\{l\}}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]\\
\;\;\;\;=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{1}{k!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1},l_{2},\ldots,l_{k}}{\Delta}R{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{1}^{1}}^{[1]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2}\}i_{1}^{2}}^{[1]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k}\}i_{1}^{k}}^{[1]}\end{array}}}

where:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\Delta}R{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{\backprime},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\begin{array}[]{rl}&\!\!\!\!=\left({W}_{+}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}_{+}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}_{+}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}-{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\right)\ldots\\
&\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\ldots\\
&\ldots{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime},i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\Delta}{\bf G}_{K}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right),{\bf G}_{R}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right)\right>\end{array}\\
\end{array}}}

given:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\Delta}{\bf G}_{K}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\ldots=\left({W}_{+}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}_{+}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}_{+}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}-{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+}\right)\\
\end{array}}}

and so we find that, overall:

{{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{x}^{[1]}_{i_{1}}&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}_{:i_{1}^{0}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\Delta}b_{i_{1}^{0}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]+\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\bf W}_{:i_{1}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\Delta}b_{i_{1}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\tilde{\bf x}}_{{\mathcal{O}}}^{[0,1]}\\
{\gamma}\\
\end{array}\right]+\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\bf W}_{:i_{1}}^{[0,1]}\\
b_{i_{1}}^{[1]}\\
\end{array}\right]^{\rm T}\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\Delta}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{[0,1]}\\
0\\
\end{array}\right]\\
&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{1}{k!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{1},l_{2},\ldots,l_{k}}{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1}\}i_{1}^{1}}^{[1]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2}\}i_{1}^{2}}^{[1]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k}\}i_{1}^{k}}^{[1]}\end{array}}}

where:

{{\begin{array}[]{rl}{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i^{\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\delta{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i^{\prime}_{1}}\Sigma^{[1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime},{\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)+{W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{\backprime[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime}}{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\\
\end{array}}}

and otherwise:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{K}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{\backprime},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime},i_{1}^{\shortmid}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\shortmid}}\atop{k^{\shortmid}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\ldots\\
\hfill\;\;\;\;\;\;\begin{array}[]{rl}&\!\!\!\!={W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{W}^{\backprime[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime}}{W}^{\backprime[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime}}\ldots{W}^{\backprime[0,1]}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\ldots\\
&\ldots{\tilde{K}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}^{i_{0}^{\backprime},i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{0}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime},\ldots,i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\end{array}\\

where:

\begin{array}[]{rl}\Sigma^{[1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime},{\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)&\!\!\!\!={\gamma}^{2}+{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[0,1]{\rm T}}{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\backprime[0,1]}={\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)^{\rm T}{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[1]}\left({\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)\\

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}{\gamma}\\
{\tilde{\bf x}}^{\prime[0,1]}\\
\end{array}\right]\\
\end{array}

and:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left[\delta_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\right]_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\otimes{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[1]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\\
{W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)\\
\end{array}\right]\end{array}}}

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1},i^{\prime\prime}_{1},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\ldots={W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime}_{1}}\ldots{W}^{\prime[0,1]}{}^{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}{}_{i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{1}}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[0,1]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{0},i^{\prime\prime}_{0},\ldots,i^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}_{0}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)\\

For the next layer:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{l}{\Delta}{\tilde{x}}^{[1,2]}_{i_{1}}=\frac{1}{1!}{{\tau}}^{[1,2](1)}\left({x}_{{\mathcal{O}}i_{1}}^{[1]}\right)\mathop{\sum}\limits_{k\geq 1}\frac{1}{k!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{\!\!\begin{array}[]{c}{\scriptstyle l_{1,1},l_{2,1},\ldots,l_{k,1}}\\

{\scriptstyle l_{1,2},l_{2,2},\ldots,l_{k,2}}\\
\end{array}\!\!}{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,1},i_{1}^{2,1},\ldots,i_{1}^{k,1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1,1}\}i_{1}^{1,1}}^{[1]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k,1}\}i_{1}^{k,1}}^{[1]}\ldots\\
\hfill\ldots{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,2},i_{1}^{2,2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k,2}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right){{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1,2}\}i_{1}^{1,2}}^{[1]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k,2}\}i_{1}^{k,2}}^{[1]}\par+\ldots\\

\ldots+\ldots\\

Recall:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\bm{\alpha}}}_{\{l\}}^{[1]}&\!\!\!\!={{\tau}}^{[1,2](1)}\left({\bf x}_{\{l\}}^{[1]}\right)\odot{\tilde{{\bm{\alpha}}}}_{\{l\}}^{[1,2]}\\
\end{array}

and so:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{\tilde{x}}_{i_{1}}^{[1,2]}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\bf k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{2}}\frac{1}{{\bf k}!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{\mathbb{N}_{k_{0}},\mathbb{N}_{k_{1}}}}{\tilde{K}}^{[1,2]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,1},i_{1}^{2,1},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},1},i_{1}^{1,2},i_{1}^{2,2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},2},\ldots,i_{1}^{1,k_{1}},i_{1}^{2,k_{1}},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}\Bigg(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k_{0}}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{k_{1}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\ldots\\
&\ldots\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\Bigg)\ldots\\
&\ldots{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{1,1}\}i_{1}^{1,1}}^{[1,2]}{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{2,1}\}i_{1}^{2,1}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}i_{1}^{k_{0},1}}^{[1,2]}{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{1,2}\}i_{1}^{1,2}}^{[1,2]}{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{2,2}\}i_{1}^{2,2}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}i_{1}^{k_{0},2}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}i_{1}^{1,k_{1}}}^{[1,2]}{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}i_{1}^{2,k_{1}}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{\tilde{{\alpha}}}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}i_{1}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}^{[1,2]}\\

where, defining:

\begin{array}[]{rl}{{\tau}}^{[1,2](k)}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{a}\atop{\ldots}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{a^{\prime}}\atop{\ldots}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\right\}\right)={{\tau}}^{[1,2](k)}\left(a\right){{\tau}}^{[1,2](k)}\left(a^{\prime}\right)\ldots\end{array}

we have:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{l}{\tilde{K}}^{[1,2]}{}_{i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots}{}^{\underline{i}_{1}^{1},\underline{i}_{1}^{1},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil;{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right\}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots\left<{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[1,2]}{}_{:i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[1,2]}{}^{:\underline{i}_{1}^{1},\underline{i}_{1}^{2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\\
\end{array}}}

and:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[1,2]}{}_{:i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\ldots={{\tau}}^{[1,2](k^{\prime})}\left(\mathbb{X}^{\prime}\right){\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{:i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots,i_{1}^{|\mathbb{X}^{\prime}|}}\left(\mathbb{X}^{\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)^{\otimes k^{\prime}}\otimes{{\tau}}^{[1,2](k^{\prime\prime})}\left(\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}\right){\bf F}^{[1]}{}_{:i_{1}^{1+|\mathbb{X}^{\prime}|},i_{1}^{2+|\mathbb{X}^{\prime}|},\ldots,i_{1}^{|\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}|+|\mathbb{X}^{\prime}|}}\left(\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)^{\otimes k^{\prime\prime}}\otimes\ldots\\
\end{array}}}

noting that with this definition:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{l}{\tilde{K}}^{[1,2]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,1},i_{1}^{2,1},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},1},i_{1}^{1,2},i_{1}^{2,2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},2},\ldots,i_{1}^{1,k_{1}},i_{1}^{2,k_{1}},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}\Bigg(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k_{0}}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{k_{1}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\ldots\\
\ldots\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\Bigg)=\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{{\tau}}^{[1,2](k_{1})}\left({x}_{i_{1}}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,1},i_{1}^{2,1},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k_{0}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,2},i_{1}^{2,2},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},2}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k_{0}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots\ldots\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{K}^{[1]}{}_{i_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{1,k_{1}},i_{1}^{2,k_{1}},\ldots,i_{1}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k_{0}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{1,1}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{1,1}}\right){{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{2,1}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{2,1}}\right)\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{k_{0},1}}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{1,2}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{1,2}}\right){{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{2,2}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{2,2}}\right)\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{k_{0},2}}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots\ldots\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{1,k_{1}}}\right){{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{2,k_{1}}}\right)\ldots{{\tau}}^{[0,1](1)}\left({x}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}}^{[0]}{}_{{i}_{0}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}\right)\end{array}}}

and continuing to the output of this layer:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\Delta}{x}_{i_{2}}^{[2]}&\!\!\!\!=\mathop{\sum}\limits_{{\bf k}\in\mathbb{Z}_{+}^{2}}\frac{1}{{\bf k}!}\mathop{\sum}\limits_{l_{\mathbb{N}_{k_{0}},\mathbb{N}_{k_{1}}}}{K}^{[2]}{}_{i_{2}}{}^{i_{2}^{1,1},i_{2}^{2,1},\ldots,i_{2}^{k_{0},1},i_{2}^{1,2},i_{2}^{2,2},\ldots,i_{2}^{k_{0},2},\ldots,i_{2}^{1,k_{1}},i_{2}^{2,k_{1}},\ldots,i_{2}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}\Bigg(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}}\atop{k_{0}}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{k_{1}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\ldots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\ldots\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots,\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}_{+},{\Theta}\Bigg)\ldots\\
&\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1,1}\}i_{2}^{1,1}}^{[1,2]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2,1}\}i_{2}^{2,1}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k_{0},1}\}i_{2}^{k_{0},1}}^{[1,2]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1,2}\}i_{2}^{1,2}}^{[1,2]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2,2}\}i_{2}^{2,2}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k_{0},2}\}i_{2}^{k_{0},2}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{1,k_{1}}\}i_{2}^{1,k_{1}}}^{[1,2]}{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{2,k_{1}}\}i_{2}^{2,k_{1}}}^{[1,2]}\ldots{{\alpha}}_{\{l_{k_{0},k_{1}}\}i_{2}^{k_{0},k_{1}}}^{[1,2]}\\
\end{array}}}

where:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{rl}{K}^{[2]}{}_{i_{2}}{}^{i^{\prime}_{2}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)&\!\!\!\!=\delta{}_{i_{2}}{}^{i^{\prime}_{2}}\Sigma^{[2]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime},{\bf x}^{\backprime}\right)+{W}^{\prime[1,2]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{1}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{2}}{W}^{\backprime[1,2]}{}^{i_{1}^{\backprime}}{}_{i_{2}^{\backprime}}{\tilde{K}}^{[1,2]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}{}^{i_{1}^{\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!=\left<{\bf F}^{[2]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{2}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\bf F}^{[2]}{}_{i_{2}^{\backprime}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\backprime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\\
\end{array}}}

and otherwise:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{l}{K}^{[2]}{}_{i_{2}^{\prime 1},i_{2}^{\prime\prime 2},\ldots}{}^{i_{2}^{\backprime 1},i_{2}^{\backprime\backprime 1},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;={W}^{\prime[1,2]}{}^{i_{1}^{\prime 1}}{}_{i_{2}^{\prime 1}}{W}^{\prime[1,2]}{}^{i_{1}^{\prime\prime 2}}{}_{i_{2}^{\prime\prime 2}}\ldots{W}^{\backprime[1,2]}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime 1}}{}^{i_{2}^{\backprime 1}}{W}^{\backprime[1,2]}{}_{i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime 2}}{}^{i_{2}^{\backprime\backprime 2}}\ldots\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\ldots{\tilde{K}}^{[1,2]}{}_{i_{1}^{\prime 1},i_{1}^{\prime\prime 2},\ldots}{}^{i_{1}^{\backprime 1},i_{1}^{\backprime\backprime 1},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\},\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime},{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\\
\;\;\;\;\;\;\;\;=\left<{\bf F}^{[2]}{}_{:i_{2}^{\prime 1},i_{2}^{\prime\prime 2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right),{\bf F}^{[2]}{}^{:i_{2}^{\backprime 1},i_{2}^{\backprime\backprime 2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\atop{k^{\backprime\backprime\backprime\backprime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\backprime}\right)\right>\\
\end{array}}}

where:

{{\begin{array}[]{l}{\bf F}^{[2]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{2}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\left[\delta_{i^{\prime}_{2},i^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\right]_{i^{\prime\prime}_{2}}\otimes{\widetilde{{\bm{\varphi}}}}^{[2]}\left({\bf x}^{\prime}\right)\\
{W}^{\prime[1,2]}{}^{i^{\prime}_{1}}{}_{i^{\prime}_{2}}{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[1,2]}{}_{i^{\prime}_{1}}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\left\lfloor\!{{\bf x}^{\prime}}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil}\atop{1}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)\\
\end{array}\right]\end{array}}}

and otherwise:

{\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!{\begin{array}[]{rl}{\bf F}^{[2]}{}_{:i_{2}^{1},i_{2}^{2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)&\!\!\!\!={W}^{\prime[1,2]}{}^{i_{1}^{1}}{}_{i_{2}^{1}}{W}^{\prime[1,2]}{}^{i_{1}^{2}}{}_{i_{2}^{2}}\ldots{\widetilde{\bf F}}^{[1,2]}{}_{:i_{1}^{1},i_{1}^{2},\ldots}\left(\left\{\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil,\ldots\left\lfloor\!{\mathbb{X}^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\atop{k^{\prime\prime\prime\prime}}\!\right\rceil\right\};{\Theta}^{\prime}\right)\\
\end{array}}}

The process may then be repeated up to the output layer to obtain the
result given in the body of the paper. Note that we have reformulated
the final version in terms of Kronecker products rather than indexed
notation for the sake of clarity. Note that in the body we extract the

\eta
out of the representation.
